Appendix A
Solving the Master Equation of the Combined System (Qutrit + Resonator)

A.1
Zero Temperature

To solve the master equation, we project the density operator of the composite system onto the energy eigenbasis of the qutrit and thus introduce the operators

\hat{\rho}_{ab}(t)=\bra{a}\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{SR}}(t)\ket{b}

(100)

for
a,b\in\{g,e,f\}
; in other words, the reduced density operator can be decomposed into

\displaystyle\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{SR}}(t)

\displaystyle=\hat{\rho}_{gg}\ket{g}\!\bra{g}+\hat{\rho}_{ge}\ket{g}\!\bra{e}+\hat{\rho}_{gf}\ket{g}\!\bra{f}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\hat{\rho}_{eg}\ket{e}\!\bra{g}+\hat{\rho}_{ee}\ket{e}\!\bra{e}+\hat{\rho}_{ef}\ket{e}\!\bra{f}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\hat{\rho}_{fg}\ket{f}\!\bra{g}+\hat{\rho}_{fe}\ket{f}\!\bra{e}+\hat{\rho}_{ff}\ket{f}\!\bra{f},

(101)

and, after expanding the master equation using the nine operators, we obtain nine coupled
operator
differential equations

\displaystyle\dot{\hat{\rho}}_{gg}

\displaystyle=-\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}\left[\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{a},\hat{\rho}_{gg}\right]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\mathrm{i}\left[\epsilon\hat{a}^{\dagger}+\epsilon^{*}\hat{a},\hat{\rho}_{gg}\right]+\kappa\mathcalboondox{D}[\hat{a}]\hat{\rho}_{gg},

(102)

\displaystyle\dot{\hat{\rho}}_{ee}

\displaystyle=-\mathrm{i}(\chi_{\text{qr}}+\Delta_{\text{rd}})\left[\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{a},\hat{\rho}_{ee}\right]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\mathrm{i}\left[\epsilon\hat{a}^{\dagger}+\epsilon^{*}\hat{a},\hat{\rho}_{ee}\right]+\kappa\mathcalboondox{D}[\hat{a}]\hat{\rho}_{ee},

(103)

\displaystyle\dot{\hat{\rho}}_{ff}

\displaystyle=-\mathrm{i}(2\chi_{\text{qr}}+\Delta_{\text{rd}})\left[\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{a},\hat{\rho}_{ff}\right]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\mathrm{i}\left[\epsilon\hat{a}^{\dagger}+\epsilon^{*}\hat{a},\hat{\rho}_{ff}\right]+\kappa\mathcalboondox{D}[\hat{a}]\hat{\rho}_{ff},

(104)

\displaystyle\dot{\hat{\rho}}_{ge}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{i}\tilde{\omega}_{\text{q}}\hat{\rho}_{ge}-\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}\left[\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{a},\hat{\rho}_{ge}\right]\!+\mathrm{i}\chi_{\text{qr}}\hat{\rho}_{ge}\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{a}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\mathrm{i}\left[\epsilon\hat{a}^{\dagger}+\epsilon^{*}\hat{a},\hat{\rho}_{ge}\right]\!+\kappa\mathcalboondox{D}[\hat{a}]\hat{\rho}_{ge}-\gamma_{2,ge}\hat{\rho}_{ge},

(105)

\displaystyle\dot{\hat{\rho}}_{eg}

\displaystyle=-\mathrm{i}\tilde{\omega}_{\text{q}}\hat{\rho}_{eg}-\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}\left[\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{a},\hat{\rho}_{eg}\right]\!-\mathrm{i}\chi_{\text{qr}}\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{a}\hat{\rho}_{eg}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\mathrm{i}\left[\epsilon\hat{a}^{\dagger}+\epsilon^{*}\hat{a},\hat{\rho}_{eg}\right]\!+\kappa\mathcalboondox{D}[\hat{a}]\hat{\rho}_{eg}-\gamma_{2,eg}\hat{\rho}_{eg},

(106)

\displaystyle\dot{\hat{\rho}}_{gf}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{i}(2\tilde{\omega}_{\text{q}}+\alpha_{\text{q}})\hat{\rho}_{gf}-\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}\left[\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{a},\hat{\rho}_{gf}\right]\!

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\mathrm{i}2\chi_{\text{qr}}\hat{\rho}_{gf}\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{a}+\mathrm{i}\left[\epsilon\hat{a}^{\dagger}+\epsilon^{*}\hat{a},\hat{\rho}_{gf}\right]\!

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\kappa\mathcalboondox{D}[\hat{a}]\hat{\rho}_{gf}-\gamma_{2,gf}\hat{\rho}_{gf},

(107)

\displaystyle\dot{\hat{\rho}}_{fg}

\displaystyle=-\mathrm{i}(2\tilde{\omega}_{\text{q}}+\alpha_{\text{q}})\hat{\rho}_{fg}-\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}\left[\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{a},\hat{\rho}_{fg}\right]\!

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -\mathrm{i}2\chi_{\text{qr}}\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{a}\hat{\rho}_{fg}+\mathrm{i}\left[\epsilon\hat{a}^{\dagger}+\epsilon^{*}\hat{a},\hat{\rho}_{fg}\right]\!

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\kappa\mathcalboondox{D}[\hat{a}]\hat{\rho}_{fg}-\gamma_{2,fg}\hat{\rho}_{fg},

(108)

\displaystyle\dot{\hat{\rho}}_{ef}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{i}(\tilde{\omega}_{\text{q}}+\alpha_{\text{q}})\hat{\rho}_{ef}-\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}\left[\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{a},\hat{\rho}_{ef}\right]\!

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\mathrm{i}\chi_{\text{qr}}(2\hat{\rho}_{ef}\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{a}-\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{a}\hat{\rho}_{ef})

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\mathrm{i}\left[\epsilon\hat{a}^{\dagger}+\epsilon^{*}\hat{a},\hat{\rho}_{ef}\right]\!+\kappa\mathcalboondox{D}[\hat{a}]\hat{\rho}_{ef}-\gamma_{2,ef}\hat{\rho}_{ef},

(109)

\displaystyle\dot{\hat{\rho}}_{fe}

\displaystyle=-\mathrm{i}(\tilde{\omega}_{\text{q}}+\alpha_{\text{q}})\hat{\rho}_{fe}-\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}\left[\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{a},\hat{\rho}_{fe}\right]\!

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -\mathrm{i}\chi_{\text{qr}}(2\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{a}\hat{\rho}_{fe}-\hat{\rho}_{fe}\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{a})

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\mathrm{i}\left[\epsilon\hat{a}^{\dagger}+\epsilon^{*}\hat{a},\hat{\rho}_{fe}\right]\!+\kappa\mathcalboondox{D}[\hat{a}]\hat{\rho}_{fe}-\gamma_{2,fe}\hat{\rho}_{fe}.

(110)

The total decay rates of the coherence are defined in the main text and are reproduced below:

\displaystyle\gamma_{2,ge}=\tilde{\gamma}_{2,ge}+(\tilde{\gamma}_{2,gf}+\tilde{\gamma}_{2,ef})/4,

(111)

\displaystyle\gamma_{2,gf}=\tilde{\gamma}_{2,gf}+(\tilde{\gamma}_{2,ge}+\tilde{\gamma}_{2,ef})/4,

(112)

\displaystyle\gamma_{2,ef}=\tilde{\gamma}_{2,ef}+(\tilde{\gamma}_{2,ge}+\tilde{\gamma}_{2,gf})/4.

(113)

Note that each operator
\hat{\rho}_{ab}
lives in an infinite dimensional space since
\mathscr{H}_{\mathcal{R}}
is a Fock space. Nevertheless, it’s possible to find a closed-form solution by invoking the positive
P
-representation

\hat{\rho}_{ab}(t)=\int\mathrm{d}^{2}\alpha\int\mathrm{d}^{2}\beta\frac{\ket{\alpha}\!\bra{\beta^*}}{\innerproduct{\beta^*}{\alpha}}P_{ab}(\alpha,\beta,t).

(114)

The reader can verify that the action of the creation and annihilation operators in the operator space can be translated to some simple operations in the positive
P
-representation:

\displaystyle\hat{a}\hat{\rho}(t)

\displaystyle\ \ \longrightarrow\ \ \alpha P(\alpha,\beta,t),

(115)

\displaystyle\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{\rho}(t)

\displaystyle\ \ \longrightarrow\ \ \left(\beta-\frac{\partial}{\partial\alpha}\right)P(\alpha,\beta,t),

(116)

\displaystyle\hat{\rho}(t)\hat{a}^{\dagger}

\displaystyle\ \ \longrightarrow\ \ \beta P(\alpha,\beta,t),

(117)

\displaystyle\hat{\rho}(t)\hat{a}

\displaystyle\ \ \longrightarrow\ \ \left(\alpha-\frac{\partial}{\partial\beta}\right)P(\alpha,\beta,t).

(118)

As an example, let us use Eq.(
115
)-(
118
) to transform Eq.(
A.1
) into a scalar equation:

\displaystyle\dot{P}_{gg}

\displaystyle=-\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}\left[\left(\beta-\frac{\partial}{\partial\alpha}\right)(\alpha P_{gg})-\left(\alpha-\frac{\partial}{\partial\beta}\right)(\beta P_{gg})\right]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\mathrm{i}\bigg[\epsilon\left(\beta-\frac{\partial}{\partial\alpha}\right)P_{gg}+\epsilon^{*}\alpha P_{gg}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\epsilon\beta P_{gg}-\epsilon^{*}\left(\alpha-\frac{\partial}{\partial\beta}\right)P_{gg}\bigg]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\kappa\bigg[\alpha\beta P_{gg}-\frac{1}{2}\left(\beta-\frac{\partial}{\partial\alpha}\right)(\alpha P_{gg})

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\frac{1}{2}\left(\alpha-\frac{\partial}{\partial\beta}\right)(\beta P_{gg})\bigg]

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial}{\partial\alpha}\left[\left(-\mathrm{i}\epsilon+\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}\alpha+\frac{\kappa\alpha}{2}\right)P_{gg}\right]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\frac{\partial}{\partial\beta}\left[\left(\mathrm{i}\epsilon^{*}-\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}\beta+\frac{\kappa\beta}{2}\right)P_{gg}\right],

(119)

where
\partial_{t}P_{gg}(\alpha,\beta,t)
is shorthanded as
\dot{P}_{gg}
. By applying the same procedure to the other eight operators, we get, in total, nine coupled
scalar
differential equations

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!\dot{P}_{gg}

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial}{\partial\alpha}\left[(-\mathrm{i}\epsilon+\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}\alpha+\kappa\alpha/2)P_{gg}\right]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\frac{\partial}{\partial\beta}\left[(\mathrm{i}\epsilon^{*}-\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}\beta+\kappa\beta/2)P_{gg}\right],

(120)

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!\dot{P}_{ee}

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial}{\partial\alpha}\left[(-\mathrm{i}\epsilon+\mathrm{i}\chi_{\text{qr}}\alpha+\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}\alpha+\kappa\alpha/2)P_{ee}\right]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\frac{\partial}{\partial\beta}\left[(\mathrm{i}\epsilon^{*}-\mathrm{i}\chi_{\text{qr}}\beta-\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}\beta+\kappa\beta/2)P_{ee}\right],

(121)

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!\dot{P}_{ff}

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial}{\partial\alpha}\left[(-\mathrm{i}\epsilon+\mathrm{i}2\chi_{\text{qr}}\alpha+\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}\alpha+\kappa\alpha/2)P_{ff}\right]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\frac{\partial}{\partial\beta}\left[(\mathrm{i}\epsilon^{*}-\mathrm{i}2\chi_{\text{qr}}\beta-\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}\beta+\kappa\beta/2)P_{ff}\right],

(122)

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!\dot{P}_{ge}

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial}{\partial\alpha}\left[(-\mathrm{i}\epsilon+\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}\alpha+\kappa\alpha/2)P_{ge}\right]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\frac{\partial}{\partial\beta}\left[(\mathrm{i}\epsilon^{*}-\mathrm{i}\chi_{\text{qr}}\beta-\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}\beta+\kappa\beta/2)P_{ge}\right]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\mathrm{i}\chi_{\text{qr}}\alpha\beta P_{ge}+\mathrm{i}\tilde{\omega}_{\text{q}}P_{ge}-\gamma_{2,ge}P_{ge},

(123)

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!\dot{P}_{eg}

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial}{\partial\alpha}\left[(-\mathrm{i}\epsilon+\mathrm{i}\chi_{\text{qr}}\alpha+\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}\alpha+\kappa\alpha/2)P_{eg}\right]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\frac{\partial}{\partial\beta}\left[(\mathrm{i}\epsilon^{*}-\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}\beta+\kappa\beta/2)P_{eg}\right]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -\mathrm{i}\chi_{\text{qr}}\alpha\beta P_{eg}-\mathrm{i}\tilde{\omega}_{\text{q}}P_{eg}-\gamma_{2,ge}P_{eg},

(124)

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!\dot{P}_{gf}

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial}{\partial\alpha}\left[(-\mathrm{i}\epsilon+\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}\alpha+\kappa\alpha/2)P_{gf}\right]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\frac{\partial}{\partial\beta}\left[(\mathrm{i}\epsilon^{*}-\mathrm{i}2\chi_{\text{qr}}\beta-\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}\beta+\kappa\beta/2)P_{gf}\right]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\mathrm{i}2\chi_{\text{qr}}\alpha\beta P_{gf}+\mathrm{i}(2\tilde{\omega}_{\text{q}}+\alpha_{\text{q}})P_{gf}-\gamma_{2,gf}P_{gf},

(125)

\displaystyle\dot{P}_{fg}

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial}{\partial\alpha}\left[(-\mathrm{i}\epsilon+\mathrm{i}2\chi_{\text{qr}}\alpha+\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}\alpha+\kappa\alpha/2)P_{fg}\right]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\frac{\partial}{\partial\beta}\left[(\mathrm{i}\epsilon^{*}-\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}\beta+\kappa\beta/2)P_{fg}\right]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -\mathrm{i}2\chi_{\text{qr}}\alpha\beta P_{fg}-\mathrm{i}(2\tilde{\omega}_{\text{q}}+\alpha_{\text{q}})P_{fg}-\gamma_{2,gf}P_{fg},

(126)

\displaystyle\dot{P}_{ef}

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial}{\partial\alpha}\left[(-\mathrm{i}\epsilon+\mathrm{i}\chi_{\text{qr}}\alpha+\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}\alpha+\kappa\alpha/2)P_{ef}\right]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\frac{\partial}{\partial\beta}\left[(\mathrm{i}\epsilon^{*}-\mathrm{i}2\chi_{\text{qr}}\beta-\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}\beta+\kappa\beta/2)P_{ef}\right]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\mathrm{i}\chi_{\text{qr}}\alpha\beta P_{ef}+\mathrm{i}(\tilde{\omega}_{\text{q}}+\alpha_{\text{q}})P_{ef}-\gamma_{2,ef}P_{ef},

(127)

\displaystyle\dot{P}_{fe}

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial}{\partial\alpha}\left[(-\mathrm{i}\epsilon+\mathrm{i}2\chi_{\text{qr}}\alpha+\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}\alpha+\kappa\alpha/2)P_{fe}\right]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\frac{\partial}{\partial\beta}\left[(\mathrm{i}\epsilon^{*}-\mathrm{i}\chi_{\text{qr}}\beta-\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}\beta+\kappa\beta/2)P_{fe}\right]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -\mathrm{i}\chi_{\text{qr}}\alpha\beta P_{fe}-\mathrm{i}(\tilde{\omega}_{\text{q}}+\alpha_{\text{q}})P_{fe}-\gamma_{2,ef}P_{fe}.

(128)

It should be noted that the differential equations of
P_{ab}
are usually of the type of a Fokker-Planck equation, which also includes the diffusive terms (i.e., the second partial derivatives with respect to
\alpha
and
\beta
). However, since we have assumed that
\bar{N}(\omega_{\text{r}})=0
, there is no terms of the form

\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{\rho}_{ab}\hat{a}\ \longrightarrow\ \ \left(\beta-\frac{\partial}{\partial\alpha}\right)\left(\alpha-\frac{\partial}{\partial\beta}\right)P(\alpha,\beta,t).

(129)

Even in the case where
\bar{N}>0
, the method of the positive
P
-representation will still work but a sharp coherent state (see below) inside the cavity will broaden itself diffusively in the phase plane.

Although looking complicated, the nine coupled equations admit simple trajectories in the complex planes of
\alpha
and
\beta
. We use the ansatze

\displaystyle P_{gg}(\alpha,\beta,t)

\displaystyle=\delta^{(2)}(\alpha-\alpha_{g}(t))\delta^{(2)}(\beta-\alpha_{g}^{*}(t)),

(130)

\displaystyle P_{ee}(\alpha,\beta,t)

\displaystyle=\delta^{(2)}(\alpha-\alpha_{e}(t))\delta^{(2)}(\beta-\alpha_{e}^{*}(t)),

(131)

\displaystyle P_{ff}(\alpha,\beta,t)

\displaystyle=\delta^{(2)}(\alpha-\alpha_{f}(t))\delta^{(2)}(\beta-\alpha_{f}^{*}(t))

(132)

for the diagonal terms and

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!P_{ge}(\alpha,\beta,t)

\displaystyle=c_{ge}(t)\delta^{(2)}(\alpha-\alpha_{g}(t))\delta^{(2)}(\beta-\alpha_{e}^{*}(t)),

(133)

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!P_{eg}(\alpha,\beta,t)

\displaystyle=c_{eg}(t)\delta^{(2)}(\alpha-\alpha_{e}(t))\delta^{(2)}(\beta-\alpha_{g}^{*}(t)),

(134)

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!P_{gf}(\alpha,\beta,t)

\displaystyle=c_{gf}(t)\delta^{(2)}(\alpha-\alpha_{g}(t))\delta^{(2)}(\beta-\alpha_{f}^{*}(t)),

(135)

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!P_{fg}(\alpha,\beta,t)

\displaystyle=c_{fg}(t)\delta^{(2)}(\alpha-\alpha_{f}(t))\delta^{(2)}(\beta-\alpha_{g}^{*}(t)),

(136)

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!P_{ef}(\alpha,\beta,t)

\displaystyle=c_{ef}(t)\delta^{(2)}(\alpha-\alpha_{e}(t))\delta^{(2)}(\beta-\alpha_{f}^{*}(t)),

(137)

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!P_{fe}(\alpha,\beta,t)

\displaystyle=c_{fe}(t)\delta^{(2)}(\alpha-\alpha_{f}(t))\delta^{(2)}(\beta-\alpha_{e}^{*}(t))

(138)

for off-diagonal terms. Each diagonal term
P_{aa}
represents a single coherent state whose amplitudes are specified by the two delta functions. Plugging the ansatze into Eq.(
A.1
)-(
A.1
), we obtain the time evolution of the coherent states

\displaystyle\dot{\alpha}_{g}

\displaystyle=-\mathrm{i}(\Delta_{\text{rd}}-\mathrm{i}\kappa/2)\alpha_{g}+\mathrm{i}\epsilon,

(139)

\displaystyle\dot{\alpha}_{e}

\displaystyle=-\mathrm{i}(\Delta_{\text{rd}}+\chi_{\text{qr}}-\mathrm{i}\kappa/2)\alpha_{e}+\mathrm{i}\epsilon,

(140)

\displaystyle\dot{\alpha}_{f}

\displaystyle=-\mathrm{i}(\Delta_{\text{rd}}+2\chi_{\text{qr}}-\mathrm{i}\kappa/2)\alpha_{f}+\mathrm{i}\epsilon.

(141)

Besides the time evolution brought by the coherent states, each non-diagonal term
P_{ab}
(
a\neq b
) is modulated by an envelope function
c_{ab}
. By substituting the ansatze together with Eq.(
139
)-(
141
) into Eq.(
123
)-(
128
), we deduce that

\displaystyle\dot{c}_{ge}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{i}(\tilde{\omega}_{\text{q}}+\mathrm{i}\gamma_{2,ge})c_{ge}+\mathrm{i}\chi_{\text{qr}}\alpha_{g}\alpha_{e}^{*}c_{ge},

(142)

\displaystyle c_{eg}

\displaystyle=c_{ge}^{*},

(143)

\displaystyle\dot{c}_{gf}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{i}(2\tilde{\omega}_{\text{q}}+\alpha_{\text{q}}+\mathrm{i}\gamma_{2,gf})c_{gf}+\mathrm{i}2\chi_{\text{qr}}\alpha_{g}\alpha_{f}^{*}c_{gf},

(144)

\displaystyle c_{fg}

\displaystyle=c_{gf}^{*},

(145)

\displaystyle\dot{c}_{ef}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{i}(\tilde{\omega}_{\text{q}}+\alpha_{\text{q}}+\mathrm{i}\gamma_{2,ef})c_{ef}+\mathrm{i}\chi_{\text{qr}}\alpha_{e}\alpha_{f}^{*}c_{ef},

(146)

\displaystyle c_{fe}

\displaystyle=c_{ef}^{*}.

(147)

Given the arbitrary initial conditions, the detailed time evolution of
\alpha_{a}
and
c_{ab}
can be solved numerically. After integrating the delta functions in the complex plane of
\alpha
and
\beta
, we arrive at the general solution (in the rotating frame)

\displaystyle\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{SR}}(t)=\sum_{a\in\{g,e,f\}}p_{a}(0)\ket{a}\!\bra{a}\otimes\ket{\alpha_a(t)}\!\bra{\alpha_a(t)}

\displaystyle+\sum_{a,b\in\{g,e,f\}}\frac{c_{ab}(t)}{\bra{\alpha_{b}(t)}\ket{\alpha_{a}(t)}}\ket{a}\!\bra{b}\otimes\ket{\alpha_a(t)}\!\bra{\alpha_b(t)},

(148)

where
p_{g,e,f}(0)
are the initial populations in each energy eigenstate of the qutrit. The steady-state (complex) amplitudes of the cavity coherent states are given by

\displaystyle\alpha_{g}(+\infty)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\kappa_{\text{in}}}\bar{a}_{\text{in}}}{\Delta_{\text{rd}}-\mathrm{i}\kappa/2},

(149)

\displaystyle\alpha_{e}(+\infty)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\kappa_{\text{in}}}\bar{a}_{\text{in}}}{\Delta_{\text{rd}}+\chi_{\text{qr}}-\mathrm{i}\kappa/2},

(150)

\displaystyle\alpha_{f}(+\infty)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sqrt{\kappa_{\text{in}}}\bar{a}_{\text{in}}}{\Delta_{\text{rd}}+2\chi_{\text{qr}}-\mathrm{i}\kappa/2}.

(151)

In the Appendix
B
and
C
, we will approach the same problem with a different technique; nevertheless, the result will be identical, except that we will include
\gamma_{1,ab}
for completeness.

A.2
Nonzero Temperature

When
\bar{N}>0
, the master equation takes the form

\displaystyle\dot{\hat{\rho}}_{\mathcal{SR}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\mathrm{i}}{\hbar}\left[\hat{H}_{\text{eff}},\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{SR}}\right]+\kappa\big(\bar{N}+1\big)\mathcalboondox{D}[\hat{a}]\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{SR}}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\kappa\bar{N}\mathcalboondox{D}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{a}^{\dagger}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{SR}}+\frac{\gamma_{2,ge}}{2}\mathcalboondox{D}\big[\hat{\sigma}_{z,ge}\big]\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{SR}}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\frac{\gamma_{2,gf}}{2}\mathcalboondox{D}\big[\hat{\sigma}_{z,gf}\big]\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{SR}}+\frac{\gamma_{2,ef}}{2}\mathcalboondox{D}\big[\hat{\sigma}_{z,ef}\big]\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{SR}}

(152)

in the long-
T_{1}
limit. The operator differential equations of
\hat{\rho}_{ab}
are almost the same as before except that we replace
\kappa\mathcalboondox{D}[\hat{a}]\hat{\rho}_{ab}
with

\kappa\big(\bar{N}+1\big)\mathcalboondox{D}[\hat{a}]\hat{\rho}_{ab}+\kappa\bar{N}\mathcalboondox{D}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{a}^{\dagger}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}_{ab}.

(153)

Consequently, the scalar differential equations for the positive
P
-representations
P_{ab}
acquire the second partial derivatives mention before, i.e.,

\dot{P}_{ab}=\big(\text{terms from the case $\bar{N}=0$}\big)+\kappa\bar{N}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial\alpha\partial\beta}P_{ab}.

(154)

Since Eq.(
154
) with
a=b
has the same form as the classical Fokker-Planck equation, we use Gaussian distributions now as the new ansatze

\displaystyle P_{gg}(\alpha,\beta,t)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\pi N(t)}\exp\left\{-\frac{1}{N(t)}\big[\alpha-\alpha_{g}(t)\big]\big[\beta-\alpha_{g}^{*}(t)\big]\right\},

(155)

\displaystyle P_{ee}(\alpha,\beta,t)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\pi N(t)}\exp\left\{-\frac{1}{N(t)}\big[\alpha-\alpha_{e}(t)\big]\big[\beta-\alpha_{e}^{*}(t)\big]\right\},

(156)

\displaystyle P_{ff}(\alpha,\beta,t)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\pi N(t)}\exp\left\{-\frac{1}{N(t)}\big[\alpha-\alpha_{f}(t)\big]\big[\beta-\alpha_{f}^{*}(t)\big]\right\},

(157)

where we require that
\alpha_{g,e,f}
still satisfy Eq.(
139
), (
140
), and (
141
), respectively. Substituting the Gaussian distributions into the Fokker-Planck equation, we obtain the differential equation of the variance
N
(more precisely, the variance of the two-dimensional Gaussian is
N/2
)

\dot{N}(t)=-\kappa\big[N(t)-\bar{N}\big].

(158)

Suppose the composite system was in thermal equilibrium with the bath before receiving the drive
\varepsilon_{\text{d}}(t)
, then we will simply use
N(+\infty)=\bar{N}
in
P_{gg}
,
P_{ee}
, and
P_{ff}
. In other words, instead of building up a coherent state in the resonator, the external drive will excite a Gaussian state with a quadrature uncertainty broadened by the thermal bath. This also means that the resonator is a continuum combination of coherent states with amplitudes near
\alpha_{g}
,
\alpha_{e}
, and
\alpha_{f}
. In contrast, if the bath is in a vacuum state,
N(+\infty)=0
and a coherent state initially excited in the resonator will remain coherent.

Unlike the case where
\bar{N}=0
, the coherence
c_{ab}
also depends on
\alpha
and
\beta
now. This is because
c_{ab}
are affected by an infinite collection of coherent states around
\alpha_{g}
,
\alpha_{e}
, and
\alpha_{f}
. Nevertheless, we expect
c_{ab}
to vanish on similar timescales set by Eq.(
142
)-(
147
).

Appendix B
Deriving the Master Equation of the Combined System (Qutrit + Resonator) in the Displaced Frame

B.1
Qutrit-State-Dependent Displacement

Due to dispersive coupling, each eigenstate of the qutrit is entangled with a coherent state of the resonator. In the last section, we have found three differential equations for the complex amplitudes
\alpha_{g}
,
\alpha_{e}
, and
\alpha_{f}
of the coherent states. To make this entanglement explicit, we define a unitary operator

\hat{\mathsf{P}}(t)=\hat{\Pi}_{g}\hat{D}(\alpha_{g}(t))+\hat{\Pi}_{e}\hat{D}(\alpha_{e}(t))+\hat{\Pi}_{f}\hat{D}(\alpha_{f}(t)),

(159)

where
\hat{\Pi}_{a}=\ket{a}\!\bra{a}
are the projection operator onto the energy eigenstate
\ket{a}
of the qutrit and
\hat{D}(\alpha_{a}(t))
are the displacement operators (see the Eq.(
40
) for the definition). Intuitively,
\hat{\mathsf{P}}
entangles each projection
\hat{\Pi}_{a}
of the qutrit with a displacement operator of the resonator such that if the qutrit is in an energy eigenstate, the resonator coherent state will be displaced to the vacuum state. For the subsequent derivation, we follow the notation used in
Gambetta et al. 2008
and use

\hat{O}^{\mathsf{P}}=\mathsf{P}^{\dagger}\hat{O}\mathsf{P}

(160)

to denote any operator
\hat{O}
in the displaced frame.

In the new frame, the density operator of the composite system is given by

\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}(t)=\mathsf{P}^{\dagger}\hat{\rho}(t)\mathsf{P},

(161)

where, to simplify the notation, we will start to use
\hat{\rho}=\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{SR}}
.
In addition, if we define

\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}_{nmab}(t)=\bra{n, a}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}(t)\ket{m, b}

(162)

to be the matrix element of
\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}
in the energy basis of the qutrit and the number basis of the resonator, then

\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}=\sum_{n,m=0}^{\infty}\sum_{a,b\in\{g,e,f\}}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}_{nmab}\ket{n, a}\!\bra{m, b}.

(163)

Our goal is to find the time evolution of the qutrit reduced density operator, i.e.,

\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}(t)=\Tr_{\mathcal{R}}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}(t)\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}=\Tr_{\mathcal{R}}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\mathsf{P}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}(t)\mathsf{P}^{\dagger}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}.

(164)

By using Eq.(
159
), we obtain

\displaystyle\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}(t)

\displaystyle=\sum_{n}\Big(\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{nngg}\ket{g}\!\bra{g}+\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{nnee}\ket{e}\!\bra{e}+\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{nnff}\ket{f}\!\bra{f}\Big)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\sum_{n,m}\Big(\lambda_{nmmn}^{ge}\ket{g}\!\bra{e}+\lambda_{mnnm}^{ge*}\ket{e}\!\bra{g}\Big)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\sum_{n,m}\Big(\lambda_{nmmn}^{gf}\ket{g}\!\bra{f}+\lambda_{mnnm}^{gf*}\ket{f}\!\bra{g}\Big)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\sum_{n,m}\Big(\lambda_{nmmn}^{ef}\ket{e}\!\bra{f}+\lambda_{mnnm}^{ef*}\ket{f}\!\bra{e}\Big),

(165)

where

\displaystyle\lambda_{nmpq}^{ge}(t)

\displaystyle=\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{nmge}e^{-\mathrm{i}\imaginary(\alpha_{e}\alpha_{g}^{*})}d_{pq},

(166)

\displaystyle\lambda_{nmpq}^{gf}(t)

\displaystyle=\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{nmgf}e^{-\mathrm{i}\imaginary(\alpha_{f}\alpha_{g}^{*})}d_{pq},

(167)

\displaystyle\lambda_{nmpq}^{ef}(t)

\displaystyle=\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{nmef}e^{-\mathrm{i}\imaginary(\alpha_{f}\alpha_{e}^{*})}d_{pq},

(168)

with
d_{pq}(t)=\bra{p}\hat{D}(\beta_{ge})\ket{q}
. To arrive at Eq.(
165
), we have used the fact that

\sum_{p}\bra{m}\hat{D}^{\dagger}(\alpha)\ket{p}\bra{p}\hat{D}(\alpha)\ket{n}=\delta_{mn}

(169)

since
\hat{D}(\alpha)
is unitary.
Once the matrix elements of
\rho^{\mathsf{P}}
(and thus
\lambda_{nmpq}^{ab}
) are known, the matrix elements of the qutrit reduced density operator can be computed trivially. For example,

\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S},gg}=\bra{g}\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}\ket{g}=\sum_{n}\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{nngg}

(170)

and

\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S},ge}=\bra{g}\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}\ket{e}=\sum_{n,m}\lambda_{nmmn}^{ge}.

(171)

B.2
Master Equation in the Displaced Frame

The density operator in the displaced frame satisfies the master equation

\displaystyle\dot{\hat{\rho}}^{\mathsf{P}}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\mathrm{i}}{\hbar}\Big[\hat{H}_{\text{eff}}^{\mathsf{P}},\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\Big]-\hat{\mathsf{P}}^{\dagger}\dot{\hat{\mathsf{P}}}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}-\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\dot{\hat{\mathsf{P}}}^{\dagger}\hat{\mathsf{P}}+\kappa\mathcalboondox{D}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{a}^{\mathsf{P}}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\gamma_{1,ge}\mathcalboondox{D}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{\sigma}_{ge}^{\mathsf{P}}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}+\gamma_{1,gf}\mathcalboondox{D}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{\sigma}_{gf}^{\mathsf{P}}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\gamma_{1,ef}\mathcalboondox{D}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{\sigma}_{ef}^{\mathsf{P}}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}+\frac{\gamma_{\phi,ge}}{2}\mathcalboondox{D}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{\sigma}_{z,ge}^{\mathsf{P}}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\frac{\gamma_{\phi,gf}}{2}\mathcalboondox{D}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{\sigma}_{z,gf}^{\mathsf{P}}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}+\frac{\gamma_{\phi,ef}}{2}\mathcalboondox{D}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{\sigma}_{z,ef}^{\mathsf{P}}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}.

(172)

As for any time-dependent unitary transformation, the extra terms
-\hat{\mathsf{P}}^{\dagger}\dot{\hat{\mathsf{P}}}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}-\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\dot{\hat{\mathsf{P}}}^{\dagger}\hat{\mathsf{P}}
appear in the new master equation to eliminate the readout drive terms in the original master equation. Moreover, the Hamiltonian of the combined system (qutrit + cavity) still takes the form

\displaystyle\hat{H}_{\text{eff}}/\hbar

\displaystyle=\hat{H}_{\mathcal{SR},\text{rot}}^{\text{disp}}/\hbar

\displaystyle=\tilde{\omega}_{\text{q}}\hat{\Pi}_{e}+(2\tilde{\omega}_{\text{q}}+\alpha_{\text{q}})\hat{\Pi}_{f}+\Delta_{\text{rd}}\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{a}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\chi_{\text{qr}}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big($}}}}}}\hat{\Pi}_{e}+2\hat{\Pi}_{f}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}}\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{a}-\left(\epsilon\hat{a}^{\dagger}+\epsilon^{*}\hat{a}\right).

(173)

Note, however, we have kept the qutrit decay (i.e.,
\gamma_{1,ab}
) in the master equation for full generality.

To begin simplifying each term, we will need various operators rewritten in the displaced frame. For the cavity operators, we have

\hat{a}^{\mathsf{P}}=\hat{a}+\left(\alpha_{g}\hat{\Pi}_{g}+\alpha_{e}\hat{\Pi}_{e}+\alpha_{f}\hat{\Pi}_{f}\right)=\hat{a}+\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha},

(174)

\displaystyle\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big($}}}}}}\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{a}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}}^{\mathsf{P}}

\displaystyle=\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{a}+\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}+\hat{a}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}^{\dagger}+\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}^{\dagger}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle=\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{a}+\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}+\hat{a}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}^{\dagger}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +|\alpha_{g}|^{2}\hat{\Pi}_{g}+|\alpha_{e}|^{2}\hat{\Pi}_{e}+|\alpha_{f}|^{2}\hat{\Pi}_{f},

(175)

where we denote

\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}(t)=\alpha_{g}(t)\hat{\Pi}_{g}+\alpha_{e}(t)\hat{\Pi}_{e}+\alpha_{f}(t)\hat{\Pi}_{f}.

(176)

Similarly, the operators associated with the qutrit subspace in the displaced frame are given by

\hat{\sigma}_{z,ab}^{\mathsf{P}}=\hat{\sigma}_{z,ab}\ \ \text{ and }\ \ \hat{\sigma}_{ab}^{\mathsf{P}}=\hat{\sigma}_{ab}\hat{D}^{\dagger}(\alpha_{a})\hat{D}(\alpha_{b}).

(177)

First, we start with the transformed Hamiltonian. By using Eq.(
174
)-(
177
), we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{\hat{H}_{\text{eff}}^{\mathsf{P}}}{\hbar}

\displaystyle=\tilde{\omega}_{\text{q}}\ket{e}\!\bra{e}+(2\tilde{\omega}_{\text{q}}+\alpha_{\text{q}})\ket{f}\!\bra{f}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -\left[\epsilon\Big(\hat{a}^{\dagger}+\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}^{\dagger}\Big)+\epsilon^{*}\Big(\hat{a}+\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}\Big)\right]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\Big[\Delta_{\text{rd}}+\chi_{\text{qr}}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big($}}}}}}\hat{\Pi}_{e}+2\hat{\Pi}_{f}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}}\Big]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \times\Big(\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{a}+\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}+\hat{a}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}^{\dagger}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +|\alpha_{g}|^{2}\hat{\Pi}_{g}+|\alpha_{e}|^{2}\hat{\Pi}_{e}+|\alpha_{f}|^{2}\hat{\Pi}_{f}\Big)

\displaystyle=\tilde{\omega}_{\text{q}}\ket{e}\!\bra{e}+(2\tilde{\omega}_{\text{q}}+\alpha_{\text{q}})\ket{f}\!\bra{f}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -\Big(\epsilon\hat{a}^{\dagger}+\epsilon^{*}\hat{a}\Big)-\Big(\epsilon\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}^{\dagger}+\epsilon^{*}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}\Big)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\Big[\Delta_{\text{rd}}+\chi_{\text{qr}}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big($}}}}}}\hat{\Pi}_{e}+2\hat{\Pi}_{f}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}}\Big]\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{a}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\Big[\Delta_{\text{rd}}+\chi_{\text{qr}}(\ket{e}\!\bra{e}+2\ket{f}\!\bra{f})\Big]\Big(\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}+\hat{a}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}^{\dagger}\Big)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\Big[\Delta_{\text{rd}}+\chi_{\text{qr}}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big($}}}}}}\hat{\Pi}_{e}+2\hat{\Pi}_{f}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}}\Big]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \times\Big(|\alpha_{g}|^{2}\hat{\Pi}_{g}+|\alpha_{e}|^{2}\hat{\Pi}_{e}+|\alpha_{f}|^{2}\hat{\Pi}_{f}\Big).

(178)

Next, to simplify
-\hat{\mathsf{P}}^{\dagger}\dot{\hat{\mathsf{P}}}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}-\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\dot{\hat{\mathsf{P}}}^{\dagger}\hat{\mathsf{P}}
, we invoke the identity

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\hat{D}(\alpha(t))

\displaystyle=\dot{\alpha}\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{D}(\alpha(t))-\hat{D}(\alpha(t))\dot{\alpha}^{*}\hat{a}-\frac{\alpha^{*}\dot{\alpha}+\dot{\alpha}^{*}\alpha}{2}\hat{D}(\alpha(t))

\displaystyle=\left[\dot{\alpha}\hat{a}^{\dagger}-\dot{\alpha}^{*}\hat{a}-\frac{\alpha^{*}\dot{\alpha}-\dot{\alpha}^{*}\alpha}{2}\right]\hat{D}(\alpha(t))

(179)

for a time-dependent displacement operator. Note that
\partial_{t}e^{\hat{A}(t)}=\dot{\hat{A}}(t)e^{\hat{A}(t)}
is only true if
\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{A}(t),\hat{A}(s)\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}=0
for any
t
and
s
, which does not apply to the case of the displacement operator. Consequently, using Eq.(
179
) results in

\displaystyle\dot{\hat{\mathsf{P}}}

\displaystyle=\hat{\Pi}_{g}\left[\left(\dot{\alpha}_{g}\hat{a}^{\dagger}-\dot{\alpha}_{g}^{*}\hat{a}\right)+\left(\dot{\alpha}_{g}^{*}\alpha_{g}-\alpha_{g}^{*}\dot{\alpha}_{g}\right)/2\right]\hat{D}(\alpha_{g})

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\hat{\Pi}_{e}\left[\left(\dot{\alpha}_{e}\hat{a}^{\dagger}-\dot{\alpha}_{e}^{*}\hat{a}\right)+\left(\dot{\alpha}_{e}^{*}\alpha_{e}-\alpha_{e}^{*}\dot{\alpha}_{e}\right)/2\right]\hat{D}(\alpha_{e})

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\hat{\Pi}_{f}\left[\left(\dot{\alpha}_{f}\hat{a}^{\dagger}-\dot{\alpha}_{f}^{*}\hat{a}\right)+\left(\dot{\alpha}_{f}^{*}\alpha_{f}-\alpha_{f}^{*}\dot{\alpha}_{f}\right)/2\right]\hat{D}(\alpha_{f})

(180)

and

\displaystyle\hat{\mathsf{P}}^{\dagger}\dot{\hat{\mathsf{P}}}

\displaystyle=\hat{\Pi}_{g}\hat{D}^{\dagger}(\alpha_{g})\big[\left(\dot{\alpha}_{g}\hat{a}^{\dagger}-\dot{\alpha}_{g}^{*}\hat{a}\right)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +\left(\dot{\alpha}_{g}^{*}\alpha_{g}-\alpha_{g}^{*}\dot{\alpha}_{g}\right)/2\big]\hat{D}(\alpha_{g})

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\hat{\Pi}_{e}\hat{D}^{\dagger}(\alpha_{e})\big[\left(\dot{\alpha}_{e}\hat{a}^{\dagger}-\dot{\alpha}_{e}^{*}\hat{a}\right)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +\left(\dot{\alpha}_{e}^{*}\alpha_{e}-\alpha_{e}^{*}\dot{\alpha}_{e}\right)/2\big]\hat{D}(\alpha_{e})

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\hat{\Pi}_{f}\hat{D}^{\dagger}(\alpha_{f})\big[\left(\dot{\alpha}_{f}\hat{a}^{\dagger}-\dot{\alpha}_{f}^{*}\hat{a}\right)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +\left(\dot{\alpha}_{f}^{*}\alpha_{f}-\alpha_{f}^{*}\dot{\alpha}_{f}\right)/2\big]\hat{D}(\alpha_{f})

\displaystyle=\hat{\Pi}_{g}\left[\left(\dot{\alpha}_{g}\hat{a}^{\dagger}-\dot{\alpha}_{g}^{*}\hat{a}\right)-\left(\dot{\alpha}_{g}^{*}\alpha_{g}-\alpha_{g}^{*}\dot{\alpha}_{g}\right)/2\right]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\hat{\Pi}_{e}\left[\left(\dot{\alpha}_{e}\hat{a}^{\dagger}-\dot{\alpha}_{e}^{*}\hat{a}\right)-\left(\dot{\alpha}_{e}^{*}\alpha_{e}-\alpha_{e}^{*}\dot{\alpha}_{e}\right)/2\right]

\displaystyle=\dot{\hat{\Pi}}_{\alpha}\hat{a}^{\dagger}-\dot{\hat{\Pi}}_{\alpha}^{\dagger}\hat{a}+\mathrm{i}\imaginary(\alpha_{g}^{*}\dot{\alpha}_{g})\hat{\Pi}_{g}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\mathrm{i}\imaginary(\alpha_{e}^{*}\dot{\alpha}_{e})\hat{\Pi}_{e}+\mathrm{i}\imaginary(\alpha_{f}^{*}\dot{\alpha}_{f})\hat{\Pi}_{f}.

(181)

Since
\dot{\hat{\mathsf{P}}}^{\dagger}\hat{\mathsf{P}}=-\hat{\mathsf{P}}^{\dagger}\dot{\hat{\mathsf{P}}}
, we have

\displaystyle-\hat{\mathsf{P}}^{\dagger}\dot{\hat{\mathsf{P}}}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}-\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\dot{\hat{\mathsf{P}}}^{\dagger}\hat{\mathsf{P}}

\displaystyle=-\left[\dot{\hat{\Pi}}_{\alpha}\hat{a}^{\dagger}-\dot{\hat{\Pi}}_{\alpha}^{\dagger}\hat{a},\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\right]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -\mathrm{i}\left[\imaginary(\alpha_{g}^{*}\dot{\alpha}_{g})\hat{\Pi}_{g}+\imaginary(\alpha_{e}^{*}\dot{\alpha}_{e})\hat{\Pi}_{e}+\imaginary(\alpha_{f}^{*}\dot{\alpha}_{f})\hat{\Pi}_{f},\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\right].

(182)

To proceed further, we substitute Eq.(
139
)-(
141
) found for the combined system into Eq.(
B.2
). In particular,

\displaystyle\dot{\hat{\Pi}}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle=\dot{\alpha}_{g}\hat{\Pi}_{g}+\dot{\alpha}_{e}\hat{\Pi}_{e}+\dot{\alpha}_{f}\hat{\Pi}_{f}

\displaystyle=\Big[-\mathrm{i}(\Delta_{\text{rd}}-\mathrm{i}\kappa/2)\alpha_{g}+\mathrm{i}\epsilon\Big]\hat{\Pi}_{g}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\Big[-\mathrm{i}(\Delta_{\text{rd}}+\chi_{\text{qr}}-\mathrm{i}\kappa/2)\alpha_{e}+\mathrm{i}\epsilon\Big]\hat{\Pi}_{e}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\Big[-\mathrm{i}(\Delta_{\text{rd}}+2\chi_{\text{qr}}-\mathrm{i}\kappa/2)\alpha_{f}+\mathrm{i}\epsilon\Big]\hat{\Pi}_{f}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{i}\epsilon-\mathrm{i}\Big[\Delta_{\text{rd}}+\chi_{\text{qr}}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big($}}}}}}\hat{\Pi}_{e}+2\hat{\Pi}_{f}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}}\Big]\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}-\frac{\kappa}{2}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}

(183)

and the first term in Eq.(
B.2
) becomes

\displaystyle\dot{\hat{\Pi}}_{\alpha}\hat{a}^{\dagger}-\dot{\hat{\Pi}}_{\alpha}^{\dagger}\hat{a}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{i}\epsilon\hat{a}^{\dagger}-\mathrm{i}\Big[\Delta_{\text{rd}}+\chi_{\text{qr}}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big($}}}}}}\hat{\Pi}_{e}+2\hat{\Pi}_{f}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}}\Big]\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}\hat{a}^{\dagger}-\frac{\kappa}{2}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}\hat{a}^{\dagger}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\mathrm{i}\epsilon^{*}\hat{a}-\mathrm{i}\Big[\Delta_{\text{rd}}+\chi_{\text{qr}}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big($}}}}}}\hat{\Pi}_{e}+2\hat{\Pi}_{f}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}}\Big]\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}^{\dagger}\hat{a}+\frac{\kappa}{2}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}^{\dagger}\hat{a}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{i}\Big(\epsilon\hat{a}^{\dagger}+\epsilon^{*}\hat{a}\Big)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -\mathrm{i}\Big[\Delta_{\text{rd}}+\chi_{\text{qr}}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big($}}}}}}\hat{\Pi}_{e}+2\hat{\Pi}_{f}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}}\Big]\Big(\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}\hat{a}^{\dagger}+\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}^{\dagger}\hat{a}\Big)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -\frac{\kappa}{2}\Big(\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}\hat{a}^{\dagger}-\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}^{\dagger}\hat{a}\Big).

(184)

With a similar manipulation, the second term in Eq.(
B.2
) reduces to

\displaystyle-\mathrm{i}\imaginary(\alpha_{g}^{*}\dot{\alpha}_{g})\hat{\Pi}_{g}-\mathrm{i}\imaginary(\alpha_{e}^{*}\dot{\alpha}_{e})\hat{\Pi}_{e}-\mathrm{i}\imaginary(\alpha_{f}^{*}\dot{\alpha}_{f})\hat{\Pi}_{f}

\displaystyle=-\mathrm{i}\Big[\imaginary(\mathrm{i}\alpha_{g}^{*}\epsilon)-\Delta_{\text{rd}}|\alpha_{g}|^{2}\Big]\hat{\Pi}_{g}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -\mathrm{i}\Big[\imaginary(\mathrm{i}\alpha_{e}^{*}\epsilon)-(\Delta_{\text{rd}}+\chi_{\text{qr}})|\alpha_{e}|^{2}\Big]\hat{\Pi}_{e}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -\mathrm{i}\Big[\imaginary(\mathrm{i}\alpha_{f}^{*}\epsilon)-(\Delta_{\text{rd}}+2\chi_{\text{qr}})|\alpha_{e}|^{2}\Big]\hat{\Pi}_{f}

\displaystyle=-\mathrm{i}\Big[\imaginary(\mathrm{i}\alpha_{g}^{*}\epsilon)\hat{\Pi}_{g}+\imaginary(\mathrm{i}\alpha_{e}^{*}\epsilon)\hat{\Pi}_{e}+\imaginary(\mathrm{i}\alpha_{f}^{*}\epsilon)\hat{\Pi}_{f}\Big]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\mathrm{i}\Big[\Delta_{\text{rd}}+\chi_{\text{qr}}(\ket{e}\!\bra{e}+2\ket{f}\!\bra{f})\Big]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \times\Big(|\alpha_{g}|^{2}\hat{\Pi}_{g}+|\alpha_{e}|^{2}\hat{\Pi}_{e}+|\alpha_{f}|^{2}\hat{\Pi}_{f}\Big).

(185)

Since
\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{1},\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}=0
, we can remove a multiple of the identity operator from the first term of Eq.(
B.2
); specifically, we can write

\displaystyle-\mathrm{i}\Big[\imaginary(\mathrm{i}\alpha_{g}^{*}\epsilon)\hat{\Pi}_{g}+\imaginary(\mathrm{i}\alpha_{e}^{*}\epsilon)\hat{\Pi}_{e}+\imaginary(\mathrm{i}\alpha_{f}^{*}\epsilon)\hat{\Pi}_{f}\Big]

\displaystyle=-\mathrm{i}\frac{\alpha_{g}^{*}\epsilon+\alpha_{g}\epsilon^{*}}{2}\hat{\Pi}_{g}-\mathrm{i}\frac{\alpha_{e}^{*}\epsilon+\alpha_{e}\epsilon^{*}}{2}\hat{\Pi}_{e}-\mathrm{i}\frac{\alpha_{f}^{*}\epsilon+\alpha_{f}\epsilon^{*}}{2}\hat{\Pi}_{f}

\displaystyle=-\frac{\mathrm{i}}{2}\frac{\epsilon^{*}(\alpha_{g}+\alpha_{e}+\alpha_{f})+\epsilon(\alpha_{g}+\alpha_{e}+\alpha_{f})^{*}}{3}\hat{1}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -\frac{\mathrm{i}}{2}\frac{\epsilon^{*}\beta_{ge}+\epsilon\beta_{ge}^{*}}{3}\hat{\sigma}_{z,ge}-\frac{\mathrm{i}}{2}\frac{\epsilon^{*}\beta_{gf}+\epsilon\beta_{gf}^{*}}{3}\hat{\sigma}_{z,gf}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -\frac{\mathrm{i}}{2}\frac{\epsilon^{*}\beta_{ef}+\epsilon\beta_{ef}^{*}}{3}\hat{\sigma}_{z,ef}

(186)

\displaystyle=-\mathrm{i}C_{1}\hat{1}+\mathrm{i}\Delta_{g,1}\hat{\Pi}_{g}+\mathrm{i}\Delta_{e,1}\hat{\Pi}_{e}+\mathrm{i}\Delta_{f,1}\hat{\Pi}_{f},

(187)

where we have defined
C_{1}=[\epsilon^{*}(\alpha_{g}+\alpha_{e}+\alpha_{f})+\epsilon(\alpha_{g}+\alpha_{e}+\alpha_{f})^{*}]/6
,

\displaystyle\beta_{ge}(t)

\displaystyle=\alpha_{g}(t)-\alpha_{e}(t),

(188)

\displaystyle\beta_{gf}(t)

\displaystyle=\alpha_{g}(t)-\alpha_{f}(t),

(189)

\displaystyle\beta_{ef}(t)

\displaystyle=\alpha_{e}(t)-\alpha_{f}(t),

(190)

and

\displaystyle\Delta_{g,1}(t)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{6}\Big[-\Big(\epsilon^{*}\beta_{ge}+\epsilon\beta_{ge}^{*}\Big)-\Big(\epsilon^{*}\beta_{gf}+\epsilon\beta_{gf}^{*}\Big)\Big],

(191)

\displaystyle\Delta_{e,1}(t)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{6}\Big[+\Big(\epsilon^{*}\beta_{ge}+\epsilon\beta_{ge}^{*}\Big)-\Big(\epsilon^{*}\beta_{ef}+\epsilon\beta_{ef}^{*}\Big)\Big],

(192)

\displaystyle\Delta_{f,1}(t)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{6}\Big[+\Big(\epsilon^{*}\beta_{gf}+\epsilon\beta_{gf}^{*}\Big)+\Big(\epsilon^{*}\beta_{ef}+\epsilon\beta_{ef}^{*}\Big)\Big].

(193)

In addition, the same argument can be applied to terms in
\hat{H}_{\text{eff}}^{\mathsf{P}}
, i.e.,

\displaystyle\epsilon\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}^{\dagger}+\epsilon^{*}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle=2C_{1}\hat{1}-2\Delta_{g,1}\hat{\Pi}_{g}-2\Delta_{e,1}\hat{\Pi}_{e}-2\Delta_{f,1}\hat{\Pi}_{f},

(194)

and the net effect is that

\displaystyle-\frac{\mathrm{i}}{\hbar}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{H}_{\text{eff}}^{\mathsf{P}},\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}-\hat{\mathsf{P}}^{\dagger}\dot{\hat{\mathsf{P}}}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}-\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\dot{\hat{\mathsf{P}}}^{\dagger}\hat{\mathsf{P}}

\displaystyle=-\mathrm{i}\Big[\Delta_{g,1}\hat{\Pi}_{g}+(\tilde{\omega}_{\text{q}}+\Delta_{e,1})\hat{\Pi}_{e}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +(2\tilde{\omega}_{\text{q}}+\Delta_{f,1})+\alpha_{\text{q}})\hat{\Pi}_{f},\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\Big]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -\mathrm{i}\Big[\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\Delta_{\text{rd}}+\chi_{\text{qr}}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big($}}}}}}\hat{\Pi}_{e}+2\hat{\Pi}_{f}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{a},\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\Big]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\frac{\kappa}{2}\Big[\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}\hat{a}^{\dagger}-\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}^{\dagger}\hat{a},\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\Big].

(195)

Now, we focus our attention on the cavity decay term

\displaystyle\mathcalboondox{D}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{a}^{\mathsf{P}}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}

\displaystyle=\Big(\hat{a}+\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}\Big)\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\Big(\hat{a}^{\dagger}+\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}^{\dagger}\Big)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -\frac{1}{2}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\Big(\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{a}+\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}+\hat{a}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}^{\dagger}+\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}^{\dagger}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}\Big)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -\frac{1}{2}\Big(\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{a}+\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}+\hat{a}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}^{\dagger}+\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}^{\dagger}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}\Big)\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}

\displaystyle=\mathcalboondox{D}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{a}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}+\mathcalboondox{D}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}+\hat{a}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\hat{\Pi}^{\dagger}+\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\hat{\Pi}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -\frac{1}{2}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}-\frac{1}{2}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\hat{a}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}^{\dagger}-\frac{1}{2}\hat{a}^{\dagger}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}-\frac{1}{2}\hat{a}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}^{\dagger}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}.

(196)

The second term
\mathcalboondox{D}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}
contains both frequency shifts and dephasing. To separate the two effects, we can simply expand the expression in the energy eigenbasis of the qutrit. For example,

\displaystyle\bra{g}\mathcalboondox{D}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\ket{e}

\displaystyle=\Big(\alpha_{g}\alpha_{e}^{*}-\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{g}|^{2}-\frac{1}{2}|\alpha_{e}|^{2}\Big)\bra{g}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\ket{e}

\displaystyle=\Big(-\frac{1}{2}|\beta_{ge}|^{2}-\mathrm{i}\imaginary(\alpha_{e}\alpha_{g}^{*})\Big)\bra{g}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\ket{e}.

(197)

By applying the same calculation to the other off-diagonal terms and noting that the diagonal terms vanish in the chosen basis, we find that

\displaystyle\mathcalboondox{D}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\Gamma_{\text{m},ge}}{4\kappa}\mathcalboondox{D}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{\sigma}_{z,ge}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}+\frac{\Gamma_{\text{m},gf}}{4\kappa}|\beta_{gf}|^{2}\mathcalboondox{D}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{\sigma}_{z,gf}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\frac{\Gamma_{\text{m},ef}}{4\kappa}|\beta_{ef}|^{2}\mathcalboondox{D}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{\sigma}_{z,ef}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -\frac{\mathrm{i}}{2}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\imaginary(\alpha_{e}\alpha_{g}^{*})\hat{\sigma}_{z,ge}+\imaginary(\alpha_{f}\alpha_{g}^{*})\hat{\sigma}_{z,gf}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +\imaginary(\alpha_{f}\alpha_{e}^{*})\hat{\sigma}_{z,ef},\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}},

(198)

where we have introduced three dephasing rates

\displaystyle\Gamma_{\text{m},ge}

\displaystyle=\kappa|\beta_{ge}|^{2},

(199)

\displaystyle\Gamma_{\text{m},gf}

\displaystyle=\kappa|\beta_{gf}|^{2},

(200)

\displaystyle\Gamma_{\text{m},ef}

\displaystyle=\kappa|\beta_{ef}|^{2}.

(201)

With the help of Eq.(

\displaystyle\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}

\displaystyle=(\alpha_{g}+\alpha_{e}+\alpha_{f})\hat{1}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\frac{\beta_{ge}}{3}\hat{\sigma}_{z,ge}+\frac{\beta_{gf}}{3}\hat{\sigma}_{z,gf}+\frac{\beta_{ef}}{3}\hat{\sigma}_{z,ef},

(202)

we find

\displaystyle\mathcalboondox{D}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{a}^{\mathsf{P}}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}

\displaystyle=\mathcalboondox{D}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{a}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}-\frac{1}{2}\Big[\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}\hat{a}^{\dagger}-\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}^{\dagger}\hat{a},\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\Big]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\kappa\mathcalboondox{D}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{a}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}+\frac{\kappa\beta_{ge}^{*}}{3}\hat{a}\Big[\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}},\hat{\sigma}_{z,ge}\Big]+\frac{\kappa\beta_{gf}^{*}}{3}\hat{a}\Big[\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}},\hat{\sigma}_{z,gf}\Big]
