\displaystyle\hat{H}^{\prime}_{\text{q,eff}}/\hbar

\displaystyle=\Delta_{g,1}\hat{\Pi}_{g}+(\tilde{\omega}_{\text{q}}+\Delta_{e,1})\hat{\Pi}_{e}+(2\tilde{\omega}_{\text{q}}+\Delta_{f,1}+\alpha_{\text{q}})\hat{\Pi}_{f}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\frac{\kappa}{2}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\imaginary(\alpha_{e}\alpha_{g}^{*})\hat{\sigma}_{z,ge}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +\imaginary(\alpha_{f}\alpha_{g}^{*})\hat{\sigma}_{z,gf}+\imaginary(\alpha_{f}\alpha_{e}^{*})\hat{\sigma}_{z,ef}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}

\displaystyle\doteq\tilde{\omega}_{g}^{\prime}\hat{\Pi}_{g}+\tilde{\omega}_{e}^{\prime}\hat{\Pi}_{e}+\tilde{\omega}_{f}^{\prime}\hat{\Pi}_{f}

(205)

to be the effective qubit Hamiltonian in the displaced frame. Note that
\hat{H}^{\prime}_{\text{q,eff}}
and
\omega_{ba}^{\prime}=\omega_{b}^{\prime}-\omega_{a}^{\prime}
are not the final effective Hamiltonian and transition frequencies of the qutrit since we are still in the displaced frame; transforming back to the laboratory frame will cancel some of the shifts seen in the displaced frame.

Appendix C
Derivation of the Effective Qutrit Master Equation

In the last section, we have established the connection between the matrix elements of the density operator in the displaced frame to the qutrit density operator in the laboratory frame. To find the effective master equation of the qutrit, we first rewrite the master equation in the displaced frame in terms of the matrix elements of
\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}
:

\displaystyle\dot{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}_{nmgg}

\displaystyle=\big[-\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}(n-m)-\kappa(n+m)/2\big]\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{nmgg}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\gamma_{1,ge}\sum_{p,q}d_{pn}^{*}d_{qm}\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{pqee}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\gamma_{1,gf}\sum_{p,q}d_{pn}^{*}d_{qm}\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{pqff}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\kappa\sqrt{(n+1)(m+1)}\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{(n+1)(m+1)gg},

(206)

\displaystyle\dot{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}_{nmee}

\displaystyle=\big[-\mathrm{i}(\Delta_{\text{rd}}+\chi_{\text{qr}})(n-m)-\gamma_{1,ge}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\kappa(n+m)/2\big]\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{nmee}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\gamma_{1,ef}\sum_{p,q}d_{pn}^{*}d_{qm}\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{pqff}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\kappa\sqrt{(n+1)(m+1)}\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{(n+1)(m+1)ee},

(207)

\displaystyle\dot{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}_{nmff}

\displaystyle=\big[-\mathrm{i}(\Delta_{\text{rd}}+2\chi_{\text{qr}})(n-m)-(\gamma_{1,gf}+\gamma_{1,ef})

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\kappa(n+m)/2\big]\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{nmff}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\kappa\sqrt{(n+1)(m+1)}\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{(n+1)(m+1)ff},

(208)

\displaystyle\dot{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}_{nmge}

\displaystyle=\big[\mathrm{i}\tilde{\omega}_{eg}^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}(n-m)+\mathrm{i}\chi_{\text{qr}}m

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\gamma_{2,ge}-\kappa(n+m)/2-\Gamma_{m,ge}\big]\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{nmge}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\kappa\sqrt{(n+1)(m+1)}\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{(n+1)(m+1)ge}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -\frac{2\kappa\beta_{ge}^{*}}{3}\sqrt{n+1}\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{(n+1)mge}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\frac{2\kappa\beta_{ge}}{3}\sqrt{m+1}\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{n(m+1)ge},

(209)

\displaystyle\dot{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}_{nmgf}

\displaystyle=\big[\mathrm{i}\tilde{\omega}_{fg}^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}(n-m)+\mathrm{i}\chi_{\text{qr}}2m

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\gamma_{2,gf}-\kappa(n+m)/2-\Gamma_{m,gf}\big]\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{nmgf}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\kappa\sqrt{(n+1)(m+1)}\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{(n+1)(m+1)gf}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -\frac{2\kappa\beta_{gf}^{*}}{3}\sqrt{n+1}\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{(n+1)mgf}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\frac{2\kappa\beta_{gf}}{3}\sqrt{m+1}\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{n(m+1)gf},

(210)

\displaystyle\dot{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}_{nmef}

\displaystyle=\big[\mathrm{i}\tilde{\omega}_{fe}^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}(n-m)+\mathrm{i}\chi_{\text{qr}}(2m-n)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\gamma_{2,ef}-\kappa(n+m)/2-\Gamma_{m,ef}\big]\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{nmef}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\kappa\sqrt{(n+1)(m+1)}\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{(n+1)(m+1)ef}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -\frac{2\kappa\beta_{ef}^{*}}{3}\sqrt{n+1}\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{(n+1)mef}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\frac{2\kappa\beta_{ef}}{3}\sqrt{m+1}\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{n(m+1)ef}.

(211)

There are three other differential equations, but they are simply the complex conjugates of Eq.(
209
)-(
211
).
Now, given Eq.(
206
)-(
208
), the differential equations governing the time evolution of the diagonal matrix elements of
\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}
(i.e., the populations of the qutrit eigenstates) are found to be

\displaystyle\dot{\rho}_{\mathcal{S},gg}

\displaystyle=\sum_{n}\dot{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}_{nngg}

\displaystyle=\gamma_{1,ge}\sum_{p,q}\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{pqee}\sum_{n}d_{pn}^{*}d_{qn}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\gamma_{1,gf}\sum_{p,q}\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{pqff}\sum_{n}d_{pn}^{*}d_{qn}

\displaystyle=\gamma_{1,ge}\rho_{\mathcal{S},ee}+\gamma_{1,gf}\rho_{\mathcal{S},ff},

(212)

\displaystyle\dot{\rho}_{\mathcal{S},ee}

\displaystyle=\sum_{n}\dot{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}_{nnee}

\displaystyle=-\gamma_{1,ge}\sum_{n}\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{nnee}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\gamma_{1,ef}\sum_{p,q}\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{pqff}\sum_{n}d_{pn}^{*}d_{qn}

\displaystyle=-\gamma_{1,ge}\rho_{\mathcal{S},ee}+\gamma_{1,ef}\rho_{\mathcal{S},ff},

(213)

\displaystyle\dot{\rho}_{\mathcal{S},ff}

\displaystyle=\sum_{n}\dot{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}_{nnff}

\displaystyle=-(\gamma_{1,gf}+\gamma_{1,ef})\sum_{n}\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{nnff}

\displaystyle=-(\gamma_{1,gf}+\gamma_{1,ef})\rho_{\mathcal{S},ff}.

(214)

Next, according to Eq.(
165
), computing the off-diagonal terms of
\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}
also requires us to know all
\lambda_{nmpq}
, whose time derivatives follow (see Eq.(
166
)-(
168
))

\displaystyle\dot{\lambda}_{nmpq}^{ge}

\displaystyle=\dot{\rho}_{nmge}^{\mathsf{P}}d_{p,q}e^{-\mathrm{i}\imaginary(\alpha_{e}\alpha_{g}^{*})}-\mathrm{i}\frac{\mathrm{d}\imaginary(\alpha_{e}\alpha_{g}^{*})}{\mathrm{d}t}\lambda_{nmpq}^{ge}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\sqrt{p}\dot{\beta}_{ge}\lambda_{nm(p-1)q}^{ge}-\sqrt{q}\dot{\beta}_{ge}^{*}\lambda_{nmp(q-1)}^{ge}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -\frac{\dot{\beta}_{ge}^{*}\beta_{ge}+\dot{\beta}_{ge}\beta_{ge}^{*}}{2}\lambda_{nmpq}^{ge},

(215)

\displaystyle\dot{\lambda}_{nmpq}^{gf}

\displaystyle=\dot{\rho}_{nmgf}^{\mathsf{P}}d_{p,q}e^{-\mathrm{i}\imaginary(\alpha_{f}\alpha_{g}^{*})}-\mathrm{i}\frac{\mathrm{d}\imaginary(\alpha_{f}\alpha_{g}^{*})}{\mathrm{d}t}\lambda_{nmpq}^{gf}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\sqrt{p}\dot{\beta}_{gf}\lambda_{nm(p-1)q}^{gf}-\sqrt{q}\dot{\beta}_{gf}^{*}\lambda_{nmp(q-1)}^{gf}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -\frac{\dot{\beta}_{gf}^{*}\beta_{gf}+\dot{\beta}_{gf}\beta_{gf}^{*}}{2}\lambda_{nmpq}^{gf},

(216)

\displaystyle\dot{\lambda}_{nmpq}^{ef}

\displaystyle=\dot{\rho}_{nmef}^{\mathsf{P}}d_{p,q}e^{-\mathrm{i}\imaginary(\alpha_{f}\alpha_{e}^{*})}-\mathrm{i}\frac{\mathrm{d}\imaginary(\alpha_{f}\alpha_{e}^{*})}{\mathrm{d}t}\lambda_{nmpq}^{ef}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\sqrt{p}\dot{\beta}_{ef}\lambda_{nm(p-1)q}^{ef}-\sqrt{q}\dot{\beta}_{ef}^{*}\lambda_{nmp(q-1)}^{ef}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -\frac{\dot{\beta}_{ef}^{*}\beta_{ef}+\dot{\beta}_{ef}\beta_{ef}^{*}}{2}\lambda_{nmpq}^{ef}.

(217)

The three equations take the same forms; we show the simplification of Eq.(
C
) as an example. Since we have a differential equation for
\rho_{nmge}^{\mathsf{P}}
, the first term on the RHS of Eq.(
C
) reduces to

\displaystyle\dot{\rho}_{nmge}^{\mathsf{P}}d_{p,q}e^{-\mathrm{i}\imaginary(\alpha_{e}\alpha_{g}^{*})}

\displaystyle=\Big[\mathrm{i}\tilde{\omega}_{eg}^{\prime}-\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}(n-m)+\mathrm{i}\chi_{\text{qr}}m

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\gamma_{2,ge}-\kappa(n+m)/2-\Gamma_{\text{m},ge}/2\Big]\lambda_{nmpq}^{ge}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\kappa\sqrt{(n+1)(m+1)}\lambda_{(n+1)(m+1)pq}^{ge}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -\frac{2\kappa\beta_{ge}^{*}}{3}\sqrt{n+1}\lambda_{(n+1)mpq}^{ge}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\frac{2\kappa\beta_{ge}}{3}\sqrt{m+1}\lambda_{n(m+1)pq}^{ge}.

(218)

To simplify the second term on the RHS of Eq.(
C
), we first compute

\displaystyle\dot{\alpha}_{e}\alpha_{g}^{*}+\alpha_{e}\dot{\alpha_{g}}^{*}

\displaystyle=\Big[-\mathrm{i}(\Delta_{\text{rd}}+\chi_{\text{qr}}-\mathrm{i}\kappa/2)\alpha_{e}+\mathrm{i}\epsilon\Big]\alpha_{g}^{*}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\alpha_{e}\Big[-\mathrm{i}(\Delta_{\text{rd}}-\mathrm{i}\kappa/2)\alpha_{g}+\mathrm{i}\epsilon\Big]^{*}

\displaystyle=-\mathrm{i}\chi_{\text{qr}}\alpha_{e}\alpha_{g}^{*}-\kappa\alpha_{e}\alpha_{g}^{*}+\mathrm{i}(\epsilon\alpha_{g}^{*}-\epsilon^{*}\alpha_{e}).

(219)

Then,

\displaystyle-\mathrm{i}\frac{\mathrm{d}\imaginary(\alpha_{e}\alpha_{g}^{*})}{\mathrm{d}t}\lambda_{nmpq}^{ge}

\displaystyle=-\mathrm{i}\imaginary\!\Big[-\mathrm{i}\chi_{\text{qr}}\alpha_{e}\alpha_{g}^{*}-\kappa\alpha_{e}\alpha_{g}^{*}+\mathrm{i}(\epsilon\alpha_{g}^{*}-\epsilon^{*}\alpha_{e})\Big]\lambda_{nmpq}^{ge}

\displaystyle=\mathrm{i}\Big[\chi_{\text{qr}}\real(\alpha_{e}\alpha_{g}^{*})+\kappa\imaginary(\alpha_{e}\alpha_{g}^{*})-\frac{\epsilon^{*}\beta_{ge}+\epsilon\beta_{ge}^{*}}{2}\Big]\lambda_{nmpq}^{ge}.

(220)

We keep the terms involving
\lambda_{nm(p-1)q}^{ge}
and
\lambda_{nmp(q-1)}^{ge}
untouched and directly go to the last term on the RHS of Eq.(
C
). Using

\displaystyle\dot{\beta}_{ge}\beta_{ge}^{*}

\displaystyle=(\dot{\alpha}_{g}-\dot{\alpha}_{e})\beta_{ge}^{*}

\displaystyle=-\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}|\beta_{ge}|^{2}-\frac{\kappa|\beta_{ge}|^{2}}{2}+\mathrm{i}\chi_{\text{qr}}\alpha_{e}\beta_{ge}^{*},

(221)

we obtain

\displaystyle-\frac{\dot{\beta}_{ge}^{*}\beta_{ge}+\dot{\beta}_{ge}\beta_{ge}^{*}}{2}\lambda_{nmpq}^{ge}

\displaystyle=\left[\frac{\kappa|\beta_{ge}|^{2}}{2}+\chi_{\text{qr}}\imaginary(\alpha_{e}\alpha_{g}^{*})\right]\lambda_{nmpq}^{ge}.

(222)

Finally, combining all the pieces yields

\displaystyle\dot{\lambda}_{nmpq}^{ge}

\displaystyle=\Big[\mathrm{i}\bar{\omega}_{eg}-\gamma_{2,ge}+\chi_{\text{qr}}\imaginary(\alpha_{e}\alpha_{g}^{*})

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}(n-m)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +\mathrm{i}\chi_{\text{qr}}m-\kappa(n+m)/2\Big]\lambda_{nmpq}^{ge}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\kappa\sqrt{(n+1)(m+1)}\lambda_{(n+1)(m+1)pq}^{ge}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -\frac{2\kappa\beta_{ge}^{*}}{3}\sqrt{n+1}\lambda_{(n+1)mpq}^{ge}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\frac{2\kappa\beta_{ge}}{3}\sqrt{m+1}\lambda_{n(m+1)pq}^{ge},

(223)

where the net frequency difference between
\ket{e}
and
\ket{g}
is found to be

\displaystyle\bar{\omega}_{eg}

\displaystyle=(\tilde{\omega}_{e}^{\prime}-\tilde{\omega}_{g}^{\prime})+\chi_{\text{qr}}\real(\alpha_{e}\alpha_{g}^{*})

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\kappa\imaginary(\alpha_{e}\alpha_{g}^{*})-\Big(\epsilon^{*}\beta_{ge}+\epsilon\beta_{ge}^{*}\Big)/2

\displaystyle=\Big[\omega_{\text{q}}+\Delta_{e,1}-\kappa\imaginary(\alpha_{e}\alpha_{g}^{*})-\Delta_{g,1}\Big]

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\chi_{\text{qr}}\real(\alpha_{e}\alpha_{g}^{*})+\kappa\imaginary(\alpha_{e}\alpha_{g}^{*})

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -\Big(\epsilon^{*}\beta_{ge}+\epsilon\beta_{ge}^{*}\Big)/2

\displaystyle=\tilde{\omega}_{\text{q}}+\chi_{\text{qr}}\real(\alpha_{e}\alpha_{g}^{*}).

(224)

Applying the same procedure to the other two equations, we find

\displaystyle\dot{\lambda}_{nmpq}^{gf}

\displaystyle=\Big[\mathrm{i}\bar{\omega}_{fg}-\gamma_{2,gf}+2\chi_{\text{qr}}\imaginary(\alpha_{f}\alpha_{g}^{*})

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}(n-m)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +\mathrm{i}2\chi_{\text{qr}}m-\kappa(n+m)/2\Big]\lambda_{nmpq}^{gf}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\kappa\sqrt{(n+1)(m+1)}\lambda_{(n+1)(m+1)pq}^{gf}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -\frac{2\kappa\beta_{gf}^{*}}{3}\sqrt{n+1}\lambda_{(n+1)mpq}^{gf}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\frac{2\kappa\beta_{gf}}{3}\sqrt{m+1}\lambda_{n(m+1)pq}^{gf}

(225)

and

\displaystyle\dot{\lambda}_{nmpq}^{ef}

\displaystyle=\Big[\mathrm{i}\bar{\omega}_{fe}-\gamma_{2,ef}+2\chi_{\text{qr}}\imaginary(\alpha_{f}\alpha_{e}^{*})

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\mathrm{i}\Delta_{\text{rd}}(n-m)

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +\mathrm{i}\chi_{\text{qr}}(2m-n)-\kappa(n+m)/2\Big]\lambda_{nmpq}^{ef}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\kappa\sqrt{(n+1)(m+1)}\lambda_{(n+1)(m+1)pq}^{ef}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ -\frac{2\kappa\beta_{ef}^{*}}{3}\sqrt{n+1}\lambda_{(n+1)mpq}^{ef}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\frac{2\kappa\beta_{ef}}{3}\sqrt{m+1}\lambda_{n(m+1)pq}^{ef}

(226)

with the net frequency differences

\displaystyle\bar{\omega}_{fg}

\displaystyle=2\tilde{\omega}_{\text{q}}+\alpha_{\text{q}}+2\chi_{\text{qr}}\real(\alpha_{f}\alpha_{g}^{*}),

(227)

\displaystyle\bar{\omega}_{fe}

\displaystyle=\tilde{\omega}_{\text{q}}+\alpha_{\text{q}}+\chi_{\text{qr}}\real(\alpha_{f}\alpha_{e}^{*}).

(228)

Since we are in the transformed frame, the photon population is initially displaced to the vacuum state, i.e.,
{\lambda}_{nmpq}^{ab}\propto\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{nmab}=0
. In addition, there is no mechanism to excite
\lambda_{nmpq}^{ab}
with
n,m,p,q>0
because the three displacement operators are designed to keep the photon number zero in the displaced frame
Carmichael 1993
; hence,
\rho_{\mathcal{S},ab}=\lambda_{0000}^{ab}
(see Eq.(
171
)) and

\displaystyle\dot{\rho}_{\mathcal{S},ge}

\displaystyle=\dot{\lambda}_{0000}^{ge}

\displaystyle=\Big[\mathrm{i}\bar{\omega}_{eg}-\gamma_{2,ge}+\chi_{\text{qr}}\imaginary(\alpha_{e}\alpha_{g}^{*})\Big]\lambda_{0000}^{ge},

(229)

\displaystyle\dot{\rho}_{\mathcal{S},gf}

\displaystyle=\dot{\lambda}_{0000}^{gf}

\displaystyle=\Big[\mathrm{i}\bar{\omega}_{gf}-\gamma_{2,gf}+2\chi_{\text{qr}}\imaginary(\alpha_{f}\alpha_{g}^{*})\Big]\lambda_{0000}^{gf},

(230)

\displaystyle\dot{\rho}_{\mathcal{S},ef}

\displaystyle=\dot{\lambda}_{0000}^{ef}

\displaystyle=\Big[\mathrm{i}\bar{\omega}_{ef}-\gamma_{2,ef}+\chi_{\text{qr}}\imaginary(\alpha_{f}\alpha_{e}^{*})\Big]\lambda_{0000}^{ef}.

(231)

As explained in the main text, by assuming the frequency shifts are much smaller than the bare frequencies, we can approximate the qutrit as a Markovian system, thus writing down Eq.(
59
). Although a qutrit is used in the derivation, the result can be easily generalized to a general qudit. The reader who goes through the steps for the qutrit case should have no problem making the generalization.

Appendix D
Derivation of the Qutrit Effective Stochastic Master Equation in the Diffusive Limit

D.1
Heuristic Derivation of the Qudit SME

Given the quantum channel defined in Eq.(
80
) of the main text, we look for a stochastic differential equation by sending
\Delta t
to 0. We start with
\eta=1
so that we do not need to worry about averaging over the unobserved information; of course, such an assumption is unphysical so we will need to relax it later. To introduce random processes that capture the measurement noise, we first examine various moments of the measurement outcomes
I_{k}=I(t_{k+1})
and
Q_{k}=Q(t_{k+1})
within
[t_{k},t_{k}+\Delta t)
conditioned on the qutrit state at
t_{k}
. To begin with, the conditional expectation of
I_{k}
and
Q_{k}
are

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}I_{k}\,|\,\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}(t_{k})\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}

\displaystyle=\iint\mathrm{d}I^{\prime}\mathrm{d}Q^{\prime}\,I^{\prime}f(I^{\prime},Q^{\prime}|\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}(t_{k}))

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\eta\kappa\Delta t}\Tr[\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}(t_{k})\hat{L}_{I}(t_{k})]

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\Delta t}),

(232)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}Q_{k}\,|\,\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}(t_{k})\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}

\displaystyle=\iint\mathrm{d}I^{\prime}\mathrm{d}Q^{\prime}\,Q^{\prime}f(I^{\prime},Q^{\prime}|\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}(t_{k}))

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\eta\kappa\Delta t}\Tr[\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}(t_{k})\hat{L}_{Q}(t_{k})]

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\Delta t}),

(233)

where we have defined

\displaystyle\hat{L}_{I}(t)

\displaystyle=\bar{I}_{g}(t)\hat{\Pi}_{g}+\bar{I}_{e}(t)\hat{\Pi}_{e}+\bar{I}_{f}(t)\hat{\Pi}_{f},

(234)

\displaystyle\hat{L}_{Q}(t)

\displaystyle=\bar{Q}_{g}(t)\hat{\Pi}_{g}+\bar{Q}_{e}(t)\hat{\Pi}_{e}+\bar{Q}_{f}(t)\hat{\Pi}_{f}.

(235)

More importantly, we also have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}I_{k}^{2}\,|\,\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}(t_{k})\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{a}\rho_{\mathcal{S},aa}\left(\eta\kappa\Delta t\bar{I}_{a}^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\right)

\displaystyle=\eta\kappa\Delta t\Tr(\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}} \hat{L}_{I}^{\dagger} \hat{L}_{I})+\frac{1}{4}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1),

(236)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}Q_{k}^{2}\,|\,\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}(t_{k})\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{a}\rho_{\mathcal{S},aa}\left(\eta\kappa\Delta t\bar{Q}_{a}^{2}+\frac{1}{4}\right)

\displaystyle=\eta\kappa\Delta t\Tr(\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}} \hat{L}_{Q}^{\dagger} \hat{L}_{Q})+\frac{1}{4}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(1),

(237)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}I_{k}Q_{k}\,|\,\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}(t_{k})\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}

\displaystyle=\eta\kappa\Delta t\Tr(\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}} \hat{L}_{I} \hat{L}_{Q})

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(\Delta t).

(238)

We observe that the second moments of
I_{k}
and
Q_{k}
are nonvanishing as
\Delta t\rightarrow 0
but the correlation between
I_{k}
and
Q_{k}
would vanish for small
\Delta t
, which implies that measurements of
I_{k}
and
Q_{k}
are related to two independent random processes. We can keep computing higher moments, such as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}I_{k}^{2}Q_{k}\,|\,\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}(t_{k})\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4}\sqrt{\eta\kappa\Delta t}\Tr(\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}} \hat{L}_{Q})+\eta\kappa\Delta t\Tr(\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}} \hat{L}_{I}^2 \hat{L}_{Q})

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\Delta t}),

(239)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}I_{k}Q_{k}^{2}\,|\,\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}(t_{k})\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{4}\sqrt{\eta\kappa\Delta t}\Tr(\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}} \hat{L}_{I})+\eta\kappa\Delta t\Tr(\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}} \hat{L}_{I} \hat{L}_{Q}^2)

\displaystyle=\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{\Delta t}),

(240)

but it’s clear that terms containing
I_{k}^{2}
and
Q_{k}^{2}
are not negligible and should be examined carefully as
\Delta t\rightarrow 0
.

In the diffusive limit, we introduce two random processes,
W_{I}(t)
and
W_{Q}(t)
, related to
I(t)
and
Q(t)
by

\displaystyle I_{k}=\sqrt{\eta\kappa\Delta t}\Tr[\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}(t_{k})\hat{L}_{I}(t_{k})]+\frac{\Delta W_{I}(t_{k})}{2\sqrt{\Delta t}},

(241)

\displaystyle Q_{k}=\sqrt{\eta\kappa\Delta t}\Tr[\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}(t_{k})\hat{L}_{Q}(t_{k})]+\frac{\Delta W_{Q}(t_{k})}{2\sqrt{\Delta t}},

(242)

where
\Delta W_{I}(t_{k})=W_{I}(t_{k+1})-W_{I}(t_{k})
and
\Delta W_{Q}(t_{k})=W_{Q}(t_{k+1})-W_{Q}(t_{k})
. From the moments of
I_{k}
and
Q_{k}
, one can easily verify that, up to the first order in
\Delta t
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\Delta W_{I}(t_{k})\,|\,\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}(t_{k})\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}=\mathbb{E}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\Delta W_{Q}(t_{k})\,|\,\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}(t_{k})\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}=0,

(243)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\Delta W_{I}(t_{k})\Delta W_{Q}(t_{k})\,|\,\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}(t_{k})\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}=0.

(244)

\mathbb{E}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}(\Delta W_{I}(t_{k}))^{2}\,|\,\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}(t_{k})\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}=\mathbb{E}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}(\Delta W_{Q}(t_{k}))^{2}\,|\,\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}(t_{k})\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}=\Delta t,

(245)

Hence,
W_{I}
and
W_{Q}
can be treated as two independent Wiener processes. Moreover, since the moments of the Wiener increments are independent of the qutrit state, we can drop the conditioning above. However, note that the qutrit state depends on the past trajectory of
W_{I}
and
W_{Q}
, which is why
\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}
should always be interpreted as the conditional states. Putting it differently, according to Eq.(
241
) and (
242
), we notice that the history of
I
and
Q
are determined once we have specified a realization of
W_{I}
and
W_{Q}
; therefore, we can generate the quantum trajectories of the qutrit during the dispersive measurement by simulating all possible realizations of
W_{I}
and
W_{Q}
.

With the preparation above, we are ready to derive the stochastic master equation in the diffusive limit from Eq.(
80
). In the limit as
\Delta t\rightarrow 0
, we can expand Eq.(
80
) to first order in
\Delta t
with the caution that the Wiener processes follow the Itô’s rule, i.e.,
(\Delta W_{I})^{2}=(\Delta W_{Q})^{2}=\Delta t
and
\Delta W_{I}\Delta W_{Q}=0
as
\Delta t\rightarrow 0
. In addition, the calculation is considerably simplified with the following observation: The Kraus operator

\displaystyle\hat{K}_{IQ}(t_{k})

\displaystyle=\mathscr{N}_{k}\sum_{a\in\{g,e,f\}}\exp\{-\Big[I-\sqrt{\eta\kappa\Delta t}\,\bar{I}_{a}(t_{k})\Big]^{2}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\Big[Q-\sqrt{\eta\kappa\Delta t}\,\bar{Q}_{a}(t_{k})\Big]^{2}\Bigg\}\hat{\Pi}_{a}

\displaystyle\approx\tilde{\mathscr{N}}_{k}\exp\{-\left[I-\sqrt{\eta\kappa\Delta t}\sum_{a}\bar{I}_{a}(t_{k})\hat{\Pi}_{a}\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\left[Q-\sqrt{\eta\kappa\Delta t}\sum_{a}\bar{Q}_{a}(t_{k})\hat{\Pi}_{a}\right]^{2}\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=\tilde{\mathscr{N}}_{k}\exp\{-\Big[I-\sqrt{\eta\kappa\Delta t}\hat{L}_{I}(t_{k})\Big]^{2}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\Big[Q-\sqrt{\eta\kappa\Delta t}\hat{L}_{Q}(t_{k})\Big]^{2}\Bigg\}

(246)

to the first order in
\Delta t
. Since
\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{L}_{I},\hat{L}_{Q}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}=0
, we immediately have

\displaystyle\hat{K}_{IQ}(t_{k})

\displaystyle\approx\tilde{\mathscr{N}}_{k}\exp{
- \Big[
I - \sqrt{\eta\kappa\Delta t}
\hat{L}_I(t_k)
\Big]^2
}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \times\exp{
- \Big[
Q - \sqrt{\eta\kappa\Delta t}
\hat{L}_Q(t_k)
\Big]^2
},

(247)

which is a great simplification and a verification that the values of
I
and
Q
are uncorrelated in the diffusive limit. Next, we replace
I
and
Q
using Eq.(
241
) and (

is implicitly fixed by a realization of

and

are two independent (real) Wiener processes. In addition, the associated heterodyne records are governed by

\displaystyle V_{I}(t)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\eta\kappa}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}}\hat{a}+\hat{a}^{\dagger}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}}(t)+\xi_{I}(t),

(250)

\displaystyle V_{Q}(t)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\eta\kappa}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}}-\mathrm{i}(\hat{a}-\hat{a}^{\dagger})\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}}(t)+\xi_{Q}(t),

(251)

where
\xi_{I}=\dot{W}_{I}
and
\xi_{Q}=\dot{W}_{Q}
.

Eq.(
249
) is only the resonator part; the decoherence of the qutrit and the qutrit-resonator coupling can be added directly, resulting in

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\hat{\rho}

\displaystyle=\bigg(-\frac{\mathrm{i}}{\hbar}\Big[\hat{H}_{\text{eff}},\hat{\rho}\Big]+\kappa\mathcalboondox{D}\big[\hat{a}\big]\hat{\rho}+\gamma_{1,ge}\mathcalboondox{D}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{\sigma}_{ge}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +\gamma_{1,gf}\mathcalboondox{D}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{\sigma}_{gf}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}+\gamma_{1,ef}\mathcalboondox{D}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{\sigma}_{ef}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +\frac{\gamma_{\phi,ge}}{2}\mathcalboondox{D}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{\sigma}_{z,ge}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}+\frac{\gamma_{\phi,gf}}{2}\mathcalboondox{D}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{\sigma}_{z,gf}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +\frac{\gamma_{\phi,ef}}{2}\mathcalboondox{D}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{\sigma}_{z,ef}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}\bigg)\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\sqrt{\eta\kappa}\mathcalboondox{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{a}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}\,\mathrm{d}W_{I}+\sqrt{\eta\kappa}\mathcalboondox{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}-\mathrm{i}\hat{a}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}\,\mathrm{d}W_{Q}.

(252)

For simplicity, we have denoted
\hat{\rho}=\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{SR}}
as the state of the combined system conditioned on the measurement record. To find an effective qutrit SME, we use the displacement operator introduced in Eq.(
159
). Since we have already solved the time evolution of the density operator in the displaced frame without the heterodyne measurement, we only need to deal with terms that come from the two measurement superoperators:

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}

\displaystyle=\Big[\text{RHS of Eq.(\ref{eq:displaced_frame_me_full})}\Big]\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\sqrt{\eta\kappa}\Big[\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big($}}}}}}\hat{a}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}+\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\hat{a}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}}+\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big($}}}}}}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}+\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}^{\dagger}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ -\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}}\hat{a}+\hat{a}^{\dagger}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}}^{\mathsf{P}}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}-\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}+\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}^{\dagger}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}}^{\mathsf{P}}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\Big]\mathrm{d}W_{I}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +\sqrt{\eta\kappa}\Big[-\mathrm{i}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big($}}}}}}\hat{a}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}-\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\hat{a}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}}-\mathrm{i}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big($}}}}}}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}-\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}^{\dagger}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ \ +\mathrm{i}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}}\hat{a}-\hat{a}^{\dagger}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}}^{\mathsf{P}}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}+\mathrm{i}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}-\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}^{\dagger}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}}^{\mathsf{P}}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\Big]\mathrm{d}W_{Q}

(253)

Note that
\langle\hat{c}\rangle^{\mathsf{P}}=\Tr\big(\hat{c}\hat{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}\big)
is the expectation value of
\hat{c}
in the displaced frame.

Following the same procedure of tracing out the resonator subspace shown in Appendix
C
, we obtain, for example,

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\rho_{\mathcal{S},gg}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\sum_{n}\dot{\rho}^{\mathsf{P}}_{nngg}\bigg)\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle=\gamma_{1,ge}\rho_{\mathcal{S},ee}\mathrm{d}t+\gamma_{1,gf}\rho_{\mathcal{S},ff}\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +2\sqrt{\eta\kappa}\Bigg[\real(\alpha_{g})-\!\!\sum_{a\in\{g,e,f\}}\!\!\real(\alpha_{g})\rho_{\mathcal{S},aa}\Bigg]\rho_{\mathcal{S},gg}\mathrm{d}W_{I}

\displaystyle\ \ \ \ +2\sqrt{\eta\kappa}\Bigg[\imaginary(\alpha_{g})-\!\!\sum_{a\in\{g,e,f\}}\!\!\imaginary(\alpha_{g})\rho_{\mathcal{S},aa}\Bigg]\rho_{\mathcal{S},gg}\mathrm{d}W_{Q},

(254)

where we have used the fact that
\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{nmab}=0
with
n,m>0
cannot be excited in the displaced frame to remove terms of the form

\sum_{n}\sqrt{n+1}\rho_{(n+1)naa}^{\mathsf{P}}\ \ \text{ and }\ \ \sum_{n}\sqrt{n+1}\rho_{n(n+1)aa}^{\mathsf{P}}.

(255)

The decay terms in Eq.(
254
) are exactly the same as the ones in Eq.(
212
) and the newly appeared stochastic terms match the matrix element

\bra{g}\Big(\sqrt{\eta\kappa}\mathcalboondox{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{L}_{I}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}\mathrm{d}W_{I}+\sqrt{\eta\kappa}\mathcalboondox{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{L}_{Q}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}\mathrm{d}W_{Q}\Big)\ket{g}.

It is not hard to see that the derivatives of
\rho_{\mathcal{S},ee}
and
\rho_{\mathcal{S},ff}
take a similar form.

Next, the stochastic differential equations of the off-diagonal terms of
\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}
can be computed similarly. To avoid complicated algebra, we need to apply, again, the fact that
{\lambda}_{nmpq}^{ab}\propto\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{nmab}=0
with
n,m,p,q>0
, which yields

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\rho_{\mathcal{S},ge}

\displaystyle=\Big[\text{RHS of Eq.(\ref{eq:lambda_0000_ge})}\Big]\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\ \ +\!\sqrt{\eta\kappa}\Bigg[(\alpha_{g}+\alpha_{e}^{*})-\!\!\!\sum_{a\in\{g,e,f\}}\!\!\!2\real(\alpha_{g})\rho_{\mathcal{S},aa}\Bigg]\rho_{\mathcal{S},ge}\mathrm{d}W_{I}

\displaystyle\ \ +\!\sqrt{\eta\kappa}\Bigg[\mathrm{i}(-\alpha_{g}+\alpha_{e}^{*})-\!\!\!\sum_{a\in\{g,e,f\}}\!\!\!2\imaginary(\alpha_{g})\rho_{\mathcal{S},aa}\Bigg]\rho_{\mathcal{S},ge}\mathrm{d}W_{Q}.

(256)

Hence, by starting from the SME of the combined system, we have shown that the qutrit acquires the measurement-induced dephasing rate
\Gamma_{\text{d},ge}
after taking the ensemble average (i.e., ignoring the stochastic terms in Eq.(
256
)). Note, however, that the stochastic terms in Eq.(
256
) do not equal

\bra{g}\Big(\sqrt{\eta\kappa}\mathcalboondox{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{L}_{I}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}\mathrm{d}W_{I}+\sqrt{\eta\kappa}\mathcalboondox{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{L}_{Q}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}\mathrm{d}W_{Q}\Big)\ket{e}.

Instead, we observe that

\bra{g}\Big(\sqrt{\eta\kappa}\mathcalboondox{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{L}_{I}^{\prime}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}\mathrm{d}W_{I}+\sqrt{\eta\kappa}\mathcalboondox{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{L}_{Q}^{\prime}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}\mathrm{d}W_{Q}\Big)\ket{e}

will produce the desired terms if we let

\displaystyle\hat{L}_{I}^{\prime}(t)

\displaystyle=\alpha_{g}(t)\hat{\Pi}_{g}+\alpha_{e}(t)\hat{\Pi}_{e}+\alpha_{f}(t)\hat{\Pi}_{f},

(257)

\displaystyle\hat{L}_{Q}^{\prime}(t)

\displaystyle=-\mathrm{i}\alpha_{g}(t)\hat{\Pi}_{g}-\mathrm{i}\alpha_{e}(t)\hat{\Pi}_{e}-\mathrm{i}\alpha_{f}(t)\hat{\Pi}_{f}.

(258)

Moreover, these new measurement operators are naturally compatible with Eq.(
254
). One can simulate the effective qutrit SME using
\hat{L}_{I}^{\prime}
and
\hat{L}_{Q}^{\prime}
, but the quantum trajectories will look qualitatively the same as the ones simulated with
\hat{L}_{I}
and
\hat{L}_{Q}
.

The difference between
\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big($}}}}}}\hat{L}_{I},\hat{L}_{Q}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}}
and
\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big($}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big($}}}}}}\hat{L}_{I}^{\prime},\hat{L}_{Q}^{\prime}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{360448}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big)$}}}}}}
lies in the definition of the two quadratures in the full SME. If we use

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{R}}=-\mathrm{i}\Big[\hat{H}_{\mathcal{R}},\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{R}}\Big]\mathrm{d}t+\mathcalboondox{D}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{a}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{R}}\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle+\sqrt{\eta\kappa}\mathcalboondox{M}\Bigg[\frac{\hat{a}+\hat{a}^{\dagger}}{2}\Bigg]\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{R}}\mathrm{d}W_{I}+\sqrt{\eta\kappa}\mathcalboondox{M}\Bigg[\frac{\hat{a}-\hat{a}^{\dagger}}{2\mathrm{i}}\Bigg]\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{R}}\mathrm{d}W_{Q}

(259)

instead of Eq.(
249
), Eq.(
256
) changes to

\displaystyle\mathrm{d}\rho_{\mathcal{S},ge}

\displaystyle=\Big[\text{RHS of Eq.(\ref{eq:lambda_0000_ge})}\Big]\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle+\!\sqrt{\eta\kappa}\Bigg[\real(\alpha_{g}+\alpha_{e})-\!\!\sum_{a\in\{g,e,f\}}\!\!2\real(\alpha_{g})\rho_{\mathcal{S},aa}\Bigg]\rho_{\mathcal{S},ge}\mathrm{d}W_{I}

\displaystyle+\!\sqrt{\eta\kappa}\Bigg[\imaginary(\alpha_{g}+\alpha_{e})-\!\!\sum_{a\in\{g,e,f\}}\!\!2\imaginary(\alpha_{g})\rho_{\mathcal{S},aa}\Bigg]\rho_{\mathcal{S},ge}\mathrm{d}W_{Q}

\displaystyle=\Big[\text{RHS of Eq.(\ref{eq:lambda_0000_ge})}\Big]\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\ \ +\bra{g}\Big(\sqrt{\eta\kappa}\mathcalboondox{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{L}_{I}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}\mathrm{d}W_{I}+\sqrt{\eta\kappa}\mathcalboondox{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big[$}}}}}}\hat{L}_{Q}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\displaystyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\textstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}{\raisebox{-3.0pt}{\resizebox{291272}{786432}{\hbox{\raisebox{4.0pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big]$}}}}}}\hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}}\mathrm{d}W_{Q}\Big)\ket{e}.

(260)

Combining the stochastic differential equations of diagonal and off-diagonal terms gives the effective qutrit SME in Eq.(
93
) of the main text, provided that we ignore the measurement-induced frequency shifts. The effective qudit SME with
D>3
can also be derived following the same logic.

Furthermore, the heterodyne records used in the SME of the combined system should be modified to depend only on the qutrit operators. To wit, we use the displaced frame and the fact that
\rho^{\mathsf{P}}_{nmab}=0
with
n,m>0
again:

\displaystyle V_{I}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\eta\kappa}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}}\hat{a}+\hat{a}^{\dagger}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}}^{\mathsf{P}}+\sqrt{\eta\kappa}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}+\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}^{\dagger}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}}^{\mathsf{P}}+\xi_{I}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\eta\kappa}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}}\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}+\hat{\Pi}_{\alpha}^{\dagger}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}}^{\mathsf{P}}+\xi_{I}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\eta\kappa}\sum_{n}\sum_{a\in\{g,e,f\}}(\alpha_{a}+\alpha_{a}^{*})\rho_{nnaa}^{\mathsf{P}}+\xi_{I}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\eta\kappa}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}}2\hat{L}_{I}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}}+\xi_{I}.

(261)

Note that the expectation value in the last line of Eq.(
261
) is computed with respect to the qutrit state only, i.e.,
\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}}2\hat{L}_{I}\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}}=\Tr(2 \hat{L}_{I} \hat{\rho}_{\mathcal{S}})
. The other quadrature follows a similar stochastic differential equation and, in summary, we have

V_{I}(t)=\sqrt{\eta\kappa}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}}2\hat{L}_{I}(t)\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}}+\xi_{I}(t),

(262)

V_{Q}(t)=\sqrt{\eta\kappa}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big\langle$}}}}}}2\hat{L}_{Q}(t)\mathclose{\mathchoice{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\displaystyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\textstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}{\raisebox{-2.5pt}{\resizebox{339817}{655360}{\hbox{\raisebox{2.5pt}{$\scriptscriptstyle\Big\rangle$}}}}}}+\xi_{Q}(t),

(263)

which is exactly Eq.(
D.1
) and (
D.1
).

For simplicity, we have assumed that
\phi=0
, i.e., the cable delay is ignored. One can replace
\hat{a}
and
\alpha_{a}
with
\hat{a}e^{-\mathrm{i}\phi}
and
\alpha_{a}e^{-\mathrm{i}\phi}
, respectively, in all the equations above to account for a constant phase shift, which is included in the main text for generality.
